Notes: Dates of issue are based on announcements in Airfix Magazine, advertisements, reviews in other magazines and occasionally box styles and kit numbers. Last dates of availability are based on catalogues, but some kits did become scarce before leaving the catalogue while others remained in the shops for years after their deletion. Where a kit is noted as issued "in" a specific year, official availability was confined to that year.

1949
====
Ferguson Tractor                480/480K/1261/1317
  1/20 scale. Boxed and bagged versions known. Available from 1949 to 1959. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/8 (1950s) from 1955 to 1960 and Thomas Manufacturing (USA) in 1950s.

  Scale not known. Ships in Bottles Cigarette Box Series. Available from 1952 to 1957.

  Scale not known. Ships in Bottles Cigarette Box Series. Available from 1952 to 1957.

1954
====
Golden Hind                     1264/64/01264-5/61264-7/9 61264/00264/90264
  1/288 scale. Copy of Gowland & Gowland kit number GH-309-S. Original issue waterline on sea base. Later versions (64 et seq) full hull. Available from 1954 to 1983. Reissued in Series 0 from 1998 to 2001. Also available in Mini starter set 90264 in 1999. Included in Armada 1368 in 1956. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/2 (1950s-1960s) and in Modellbaukasten gift set 1740 (1950s).
Santa Maria                     1265/65/01265-8/61265-0/61270-2/9 61270
  Scale not known. Copy of Gowland & Gowland kit number SM-308-S. Original issue waterline on sea base. Later versions (65 et seq) full hull. Converted to Snap'n'Glue (61270-2) in 1981. Available from 1954 to 1983. Included in Armada 1368 in 1956. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/1 (1950s-1960s) and by Heller (France) as 79705 (1990s).
HMS Shannon                     1285/66/01266-1/61266-3
  Scale not known. Copy of Gowland & Gowland USS Constitution kit. Original issue waterline on sea base. Later versions (66 et seq) full hull. Available from 1954 to 1980. Included in Armada 1368 in 1956. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/3 (1950s-1960s). 

1955
====
SS Southern Cross               1298/1423
  1/480 scale. Available from 1955 to 1959. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/5 (1950s).
HMS Victory                     1306/67/01267-4/61267-6/9 61267/00267/90627/91267/01267G/A55104/A68218
  Scale not known. Original issue waterline on sea base. Later versions (67 et seq) full hull. Available from 1955 to 1983. Reissued in Series 0 from 1998 to 2001. Included in Armada 1368 in 1956, mini starter set 90267 in 1999, gift set 91267 and 01267G from 2005 to 2007, starter set A55104 from 2012 to 2021 and gift set A68218 in 2019. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/4 (1950s-1960s).
Cutty Sark                      1309/68/01268-7/61268-9/61271-5/9 61271/A55103/A68215
  Scale not known. Original issue waterline on sea base. Later versions (68 et seq) full hull. Available from 1955 to 1983. Converted to Snap'n'Glue (61271-5) in 1981. Included in Armada 1368 in 1956, in starter set A55103 from 2012 to 2015 and gift set A68215 in 2018. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/6 (1950s-1960s) and by Heller (France) as 79708 (1990s).
1911 Rolls Royce                1315/71/01441-0/02444-2/02444
  1/32 scale. Copy of the Gowland & Gowland kit. Available from 1955 to 1980. May not have been sold as 01441-0. In Series 2 from 1975. Reissued from 1987 to 1992. Included in Vintage Car Rally 1369 in 1956 and Modelling Gift Set 950a? in 1993?. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/7 (1950s-1960s), by MPC (USA) as customising kit 1003 with new parts from 1970 to 1971 and USAirfix as 8201 from 1980 to 1981.
Vickers Supermarine Spitfire    1316
  1/72 scale. Reduced scale copy of Aurora 1/48 kit number 20. Available from 1955 to 1959. Replaced by Spitfire Mk.IX 1316 in 1959. Included in Historical Air Fleet 1370 in 1956 and Famous Aircraft No.2 Set 5012 in 1959. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 9 (1950s) and in Modellbaukasten gift set 1740 (1950s) and Kellogg's (UK) Frosties Famous Fighters of WWII promotion (1960s).

1956
====
Gloster Gladiator Mk.II/I       1335/82/01002-9/61002-1/01002/A01002
  1/72 scale. Available as Sea Gladiator Mk.II "Faith" 1335 with three blade propeller and Maltese decals from 1956 to 1959, as Gladiator Mk.I 1335 with two blade propeller and 72 Squadron RAF pre-war decals from 1959 to 1963, as 82 from 1963 to 1972, as 01002-9 from 1973 to 1978 and as 61002-1 from 1975 to 1978. Reissued as 01002 from 1987 to 1988 and as Gladiator Mk.I A01002 with three blade propeller on additional sprue from 2008 to 2012. Replaced by A02052 in 2013. Included in Historical Air Fleet 1370 in 1956, 90 Years of Fighters 08655 in 2004 and RAF 90th Anniversary Collection A50029 from 2008 to 2009. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/10 (1950s).
Westland Whirlwind S-55 Helicopter 1355
  1/72 scale. Available from 1956 to 1966. Civilian Whirlwind S-55 in BEA livery. Retooled as Whirlwind HAR.1 124 in 1966. Included in Historical Air Fleet 1370 in 1956 and Aircraft Series No.2 in 1960?. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/14 (1950s-1960s).
Messerschmitt Me 109F           1384
  1/72 scale. Available from 1956 to 1959. Retooled into Me 109G 1384 in 1959. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/15 (1950s-1960s).
Westland Lysander Mk.II         1385/84
  1/72 scale. Available from 1956 to 1972. Replaced by 01004 in 1972. Included in Historical Air Fleet 1370 in 1956. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/16 (1950s-1960s).
Bristol Fighter F2B             1386/85/01005-8/61005-0/9 61005/01005/01080/00080/A01080
  1/72 scale. Minor modifications to tooling in 1963. Available from 1956 to 1976. Reissued in 1920s markings from 1980 to 1983 and 1986 to 1988, Vintage Aircraft series (01080) from 1988 to 1996, Series 0 from 2001 to 2003 and Series 1 from 2010 to 2012. Included in Historical Air Fleet 1370 in 1956 and Modelling Gift Set Vintage Aircraft Selection (Series 1) in 1990. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/17 (1950s-1960s) and Airfix Corporation of America as 7-39 (1963 to 1966).
1905 Rolls Royce                1336/75/01445-2/02447-1/02447
  1/32 scale. Available from 1956 to 1979. May not have been sold as 01445-2. In Series 2 from 1975. Reissued from 1988 to 1992. Included in Vintage Car Rally 1369 in 1956 and Modelling Gift Set 'E' in 1989. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/19 (1950s-1960s) and by MPC (USA) as Rolls Runner  customising kit 1009 with new parts from 1970 to 1971 and as 2-1019 in 1973.
1910 Model T Ford               1337/74
  1/32 scale. Available from 1956 to 1969. Listed in 1973 and 1974 catalogues as 01444-9 but may not have appeared in Type 4 box. Included in Vintage Car Rally 1369 in 1956. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/13 (1950s-1960s) and by Kellogg's in 1971 as Puffa Puffa Rice/Variety offer.
1904 Darracq                    1338/72/01442-3/02445-5
  Closer to 1/24 than 1/32 scale. Available from 1956 to 1978. May not have been sold as 01442-5. In Series 2 from 1975. Included in Vintage Car Rally 1369 in 1956. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/11 (1950s-1960s), in Modellbaukasten gift set 1740 (1950s) and by MPC (USA) as customising kit 1002 with new parts from 1970 to 1971 and as 2-1020 in 1973.
1930 Bentley                    1344/73/01443-6/02446-8/02446
  1/32 scale. Available from 1956 to 1980. May not have been sold as 01443-6. In Series 2 from 1975. Reissued from 1987 to 1992 and in 2004. Included in Vintage Car Rally 1369 in 1956. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/12 (1950s-1960s), by MPC (USA) as 2-1018 in 1973 and USAirfix as 8202 from 1980 to 1981.
Armada                          1368
  Scale not known. Includes Golden Hind 1264, Santa Maria 1265, HMS Shannon 1285, HMS Victory 1306 and Cutty Sark 1309. Available in 1956.
Vintage Car Rally               1369
  1/32 scale. Includes 1911 Rolls Royce 1315, 1905 Rolls Royce 1336, 1904 Darracq 1338, 1910 Model T Ford 1337 and 1930 Bentley 1344. Available in 1956.
Historical Air Fleet            1370
  1/72 scale. Includes Spitfire 1316, Gloster Gladiator Mk.II 1335, Whirlwind S-55 1355, Westland Lysander Mk.II 1385 and Bristol Fighter F2B 1386. Available in 1956.

1957
====
Fokker Dr.I Triplane            1387/88/01008-7/61008-9/01074/00074/A76509
  1/72 scale. Minor retooling in 1963. Available from 1957 to 1978. Reissued from 1987 in Vintage Aircraft series (01074) to 1997. Reissued in Series 0 from 2001 to 2003. Also available in bag for Airfix Challenge A76509 from 2015. Included in Famous Aircraft No.1 Set 5011 in 1959, Modelling Gift Set Series 1 Selection from 1988 to 1989 and 90 Years of Fighters 08655 in 2004 and Science Museum 20th Century Flight A50057 from 2008 to 2010. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/22 (1950s-1960s), by Airfix Corporation of America as 1-29 from 1963 to 1966, by Airfix by Craftmaster (USA) as 1001 from 1966 to 1969 and by MPC (USA) in The Red Baron's Last Dogfight 2-1204 from 1971 to 1974.
Supermarine S.6B                1391/87/01007-4/01007
  1/72 scale. Available from 1957 to 1975. Listed in 1984 catalogue as included in 9 60262 gift set but not issued. Listed as 9 02076 in 1985 catalogue but not issued. Reissued from 1987 to 1991. Included in Limited Edition Collectors Tin 06041 in 2006 and Spitfire 70th Aniversary Set 08678 from 2006 to 2007. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/21 (1950s-1960s).
Sopwith 2F1 Camel               1392/89/01009-0/01075/00075/A76508
  1/72 scale. Minor retooling in 1963. Available from 1957 to 1976. Reissued in Vintage Aircraft series (01075) from 1987 to 1997. Reissued in Series 0 from 2001 to 2003. Also available in bag for Airfix Challenge A76508 from 2015. Included in Modelling Gift Set Series 1 Selection from 1988 to 1989, Modelling Gift Set Vintage Aircraft Selection (Series 1) in 1990, 100 Years of Flight 1903-2003 91006 in 2003 and RAF 90th Anniversary Collection A50029 from 2008 to 2009 and RAF Centenary Gift Set A50181 from 2018. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/20 (1950s-1960s), Airfix Corporation of America as 2-29 from 1963 to 1966, by Airfix by Craftmaster (USA) as 1002 from 1966 to 1969 and by MPC (USA) in The Red Baron's Last Dogfight 2-1204 from 1971 to 1974.
Albatros Scout D.V              1393/90/01010-0/01078/00078/A01078
  1/72 scale. Minor retooling in 1963. Available from 1957 to 1976. Reissued in Vintage Aircraft Series (01078) from 1987 to 1996, Series 0 from 2001 to 2003, and Series 1 from 2010 to 2012. Included in Modelling Gift Set Series 1 Selection from 1988 to 1989. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/24 (1950s-1960s), by Airfix Corporation of America as 3-29 from 1963 to 1966 and Airfix by Craftmaster (USA) as 1003 from 1966 to 1969.
Junkers Ju 87B Stuka            1395/91/01011-3
  1/72 scale. Available from 1957 to 1977. 02049-1 was originally intended to replace it as 01067-6, but was issued in Series 2 instead. Included in Famous Aircraft No.1 Set 5011 in 1959. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/23 (1950s-1960s), by Airfix Corporation of America as 2-39 from 1963 to 1966, by MPC (USA) in Customising Series 2-7004 from 1968 to 1971, Profile Series 2-1107 from 1971 to 1976 and Bonus Series 2-0102 from 1976 to 1978 and by MPC/Kikoler (Brazil) in Profile Series 2-1107 in 1974.

  1/72 scale. Available from 1957 to 1967. Retooled into 132 Hawker Demon in 1968. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/44 (1950s-1960s).

  1/72 scale. Available from 1957 to 1976. D.H. Tiger Moth II              1400/95/01015-5/61015-7/01015/00015/00015G/A01015
  1/72 scale. Available from 1957 to 1979. Reissued from 1987 to 1998, in Series 0 from 1999 to 2003 and in Series 1 from 2005 to 2012.   1/72 scale. Available from 1957 to 1974. Listed as 01016-8 in 1973 and 1974 catalogues but not issued in Type 4 packaging. ====
Westland Whirlwind Mk.I         1406/99/01019-7
  1/72 scale. Available from 1958 to 1977. 02064 was to replace it in 1977 with the same kit number, but was issued in Series 2 instead. Included in 8 Aircraft Kit Set in 1966?. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/34 (1950s-1960s).
Saunders-Roe SR.53              1407/100
  1/72 scale. Available from 1958 to 1974. Listed as 01020-7 in 1974 and as 02059-8 from 1975 to 1976, but not apparently issued in Type 4 packaging. Included in Famous Aircraft No.1 Set 5011 in 1959 and MPC Special Gift Pack Savings in 1968?. Also issued by Plasty (Germany) as 1640/33 (1950s-1960s) and by MPC (USA) in Customising Series 2-7015 from 1968 to 1971. Included in Mould missing in 2015.

1986
====
No new kits were issued this year. Humbrol bought Airfix from Palitoy, and reissued 89 kits in the summer and another 39 later in the year.
